## Document 095: 2347聯強 台新

### Page 1

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

聯強(2347)：資金成本上揚壓抑短期營收動能，但長期營益率改善趨勢
不變 
建議：維持買進

一、聯強為亞太地區最大 3C 通
路商，營收成長以半導體業務最
佳  
 聯強為 3C 電子產品之銷售、配送、維修、量身訂做即時生產之專業通路商，為
亞太地區 3C 通路商龍頭，2026 年前三季產品比重分別為商用加值佔 31%、資
訊消費佔 17% 、 半 導 體 佔 48% 、 手 機 產 品 佔 4% ，成長主要來商用加值
(+112%YoY)和半導體業務(+162%YoY)。聯強海外市場包含中國、香港、紐澳、
泰國、印尼、印度及越南等地，其中以中國、台灣和印尼等區域較佳，營收分別
為 YoY+109%、YoY+78%和 YoY+18%。聯強競爭優勢為透過其四合一經營模
式，搭配其綿密的通路發揮規模經濟，並提供高附加價值的後勤運籌(庫存管理
與快速維為修服務等)，於中國主要競爭對手為神州數碼、天音通信控股。

二、半導體業務成長性佳，3Q26 
EPS 2.93 元創新高並優於預期 
 3Q26 營收 2366 億元(+18%QoQ；+136%YoY)優於預估值 14%，其中商用加
值和半導體營收分別為+116%YOY 和+273%YoY，因秉棄部分利潤不佳專案，
毛利率 3.49%優於前季之 3.4%，營業利益 61.41 億元(+28%%QoQ)優於預估
值 24%，因營運資金需求以及籌資成本上升致使利息費用提高至 9.34 億元
(+106%QoQ)，稅後純益 48.83 億元(+25%QoQ；+90%YoY)，EPS 2.93 元優
於預期之 2.5 元並創新高。

三、資金成本上揚導致業務拓展
較為謹慎，估計 4Q26 EPS 2.34
元 
 由於資金成本上揚致使公司放棄部分利潤不佳半導體業務，十月營收月減 34%，
估計 4Q26 營收 2046 億元(-14%QoQ；YoY+62%)，因產品組合變化轉佳，毛
利率提升至 3.58%，稅後純益 38.99 億元(-20%QoQ；+41%YoY)，EPS 2.34
元。

四、AI 長線需求有助商用加值和
半導體業務成長性 
 集邦 預估 2026 和 2027 年全 球九 大 CSP 資 本支 出分 別為 0.95 兆 美元
(+106%YoY)和 1.53 兆美元(+60%YoY)，且 AI 需求從雲端擴展到企業端，其中
以商用加值和半導體業務受惠幅度最大。 
在商用加值業務方面，在台灣和中國資料中心產品出貨挹注下，3Q26 營收
+116%YoY。展望 2027 年，中國十十五規劃和國產化政策推動政府和國企加速
採用本土品牌，推升聯強代理華為、浪潮等品牌業務成長性，台灣則是接獲數個
半導體大廠 AI 基礎建設專案，加計中小企業 AI 建置需求逐步顯現，商用業務將
為營運成長主要動能之一。 
在半導體業務方面，缺貨和漲價效致使記憶體、SSD 和硬碟業務為半導體業務
中 成 長 性 最 佳 者 ， 且 AI 推 論 亦 增 加 CPU 需求，3Q26 半 導 體 業 務 營 收

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報 
YoY+273%，半導體業務為營運成長另一重點業務，但因資金成本上揚，公司將
摒棄部分利潤未達目標專案，此將影響短期營收動能。 
受惠於出貨 ASP 增加，以及原廠擴大促銷力道挽救銷售量，3Q26 資訊家用和
手機業務分別+21%YoY 和+33%YoY。記憶體大漲致使原廠逐步反應成本於 PC
和手機新機售價，並將導致消費者延長換機週期，2027 年此兩塊業務成長性仍
相對保守看待。

五、資金成本上揚將致使接案更
為謹慎，但長期營益率好轉方向
不變 
 聯強今年接獲數個大型 AI 基礎建設專案，致使營收動能以及營運資金需求大增，
3Q26 遇到資金成本上揚，致使利息費用較前季倍增，公司將透過聯貸、可轉債
以及現增降低利息負擔，預期三項籌資工具皆完成後可望降低利息費用 20-
30%。展望 2027 年，儘管營收成長性不如 2026 年亮眼，但接單策略轉趨謹慎
將致使毛利率下滑壓力降低，且預期營業費用增幅有限下，營益率持續好轉方向
不變。

六、維持買進理由  1)預估 2026 和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 9.38 元和 11.07 元。 
2)儘管資金成本上揚壓抑短期營收動能，但長期營益率改善趨勢不變，維持買進
評等，TP 100 元 (2027 PE x 9)。

## Document 102: 2458義隆電 玉山

研 究 報 告                
義隆(2458.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
深受 NB 出貨趨緩影響，短期營運受壓抑 
 投資評價與建議 
NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能，無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來源，
2027年營運展望保守，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。 
 重點評論分析 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但
延續性恐不佳，僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳，推估營收16億元，QoQ-27.9%，
且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解，2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限：展望2027年，IDC預估
PC出貨量YoY-4.1%，而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關，因此短期公司營運亦
同處於逆風，主要動能來自非NB產品的無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身
演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP產品，1H26出貨
以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，
已通過雷虎測試並開始出貨，2H26 ASP明顯提升，為2026年最佳成⻑動能，
也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能，2026年AI營收YoY+40%，也是未來公司
重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而
言，短期NB客⼾拉貨恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較
受限。 
 投資風險 
NB需求進⼀步下滑或非NB貢獻不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況 
3Q26營運受NB影響而趨緩，獲利低於預期：3Q26營收22.2億元，QoQ-30.8%，主因1H26 NB客⼾提前拉貨及
缺料造成拉貨⼒道下滑，毛利率44.7%，主因產品組合轉差及供應鏈成本提升(包含模組的被動元件及PCB漲價)，
費用因嚴控成本而下滑至6.96億元，業外主要來自金融評價損失，EPS 0.58元，優於預期。 
4Q26 NB產業承壓，營運同步面臨逆風：NB客⼾面臨CPU、記憶體缺料問題，拉貨保守，雖有短/急單，但延續
性恐不佳，僅有無⼈機相關產品表現較佳，推估營收16億元，QoQ-27.9%，且仍持續面臨供應鏈成本壓⼒，推估
毛利率43.9%，EPS 0.51元。 
短期NB缺料問題難解，2027年營運成⻑動能恐受限：受記憶體與CPU缺貨持續壓抑NB出貨成⻑性，以及NB漲價、
AI排擠商用NB需求等因素，雖1H26提前拉貨推升公司營運仍佳，然自3Q26起NB客⼾需求趨緩，使得公司營運逐
季下滑，展望2027年，IDC預估PC出貨量YoY-4.1%，而義隆營收約80%以上與NB相關，因此短期公司營運亦同
處於逆風，主要動能來自1)Haptic滲透率持續提升，因Haptic ASP達15美元，明顯優於傳統clickpad 2美元，將
帶動觸控板ASP提升，2026年Haptic營收貢獻約3.5~4億元(2025年約0.6億元)，2027年也將持續提升；2)ADAS
來自電動巴士，2026年出貨double，已有第六位客⼾開始小量出貨，2027年持續成⻑，主要來自政府電動巴士
補助所推升，政府最近也補助電動物流車，2027年確實落地後，公司將有電動物流車的成⻑機會，量也比電動巴
士多很多；3)無⼈機：公司與中研院合作，利用自身演算法優勢，推出Mini LED local dimming(區域調光)與ISP
產品，1H26出貨以ISP為主，ASP約5~6美元，7月已開始出貨AI Box，ASP約300~500美元，已通過雷虎測試並
開始出貨，目前僅作前端光學模組，2026年底將推出避障功能，四鏡頭方案約500美元，十二鏡頭方案將達1,000
美元，2H26 ASP明顯提升，為2026年最佳成⻑動能，也是推升AI應用占比提升主要動能，2026年AI營收YoY+40%，
也是未來公司重心，且公司具備MIT優勢，將積極爭取國防訂單，未來具成⻑潛⼒。整體而言，短期NB客⼾拉貨
恐保守，主要仰賴無⼈機等非NB產品推升，營運成⻑較受限。 
2027年營運動能受限，維持中立評等：NB客⼾拉貨⼒道趨弱將壓抑整體營運動能，無⼈機相關產品為主要成⻑來
源，2027年營運展望保守，維持中立評等，給予30倍PER*2027年EPS評價。

### Page 1

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
Growth Intact Despite A Longer HVDC Transition
CITI'S TAKE 
We walked away from AES’s 3Q briefing more confident in the BBU growth 
outlook. Resilient low-voltage demand and higher-power product 
upgrades should sustain growth through the HVDC transition, with 
400V/800V commercialization targeted for mid/end-2027E respectively. 
Rising technical complexity should favor AES’s engineering expertise and 
established customer relationships. We trim our TP to NT$1,550 (from 
NT$1,680) on 25x 2027E P/E from 27x reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty. 
Reiterate Buy as rising BBU penetration and content per rack underpin 
structural growth.
3Q26: strong BBU demand drives growth and mix upgrades lift GPM y/y — 3Q 
sales of NT$5.2bn rose 27% y/y and 9% q/q, driven by strong BBU demand and were 
9% above our forecast. GPM expanded 1.3ppt y/y to 36.3% on mix upgrades, though 
it fell 2.8ppt q/q. Op profits rose 34% y/y with OPM up 1.4ppt y/y to 24.6%. Net profit 
of NT$1.1bn was up 34% y/y, 3% above our forecast but 5% below consensus. Higher 
opex and lower non-op income partly offset the sales beat.
Low voltage BBUs retain a substantial growth runway — Mgmt expects steady 4Q 
growth and remains positive on 2027E, with no downward revisions to customer 
demand plans. Continued datacenter buildout should sustain low-voltage BBU 
procurement while HVDC architectures undergo qualifications. Demand for 3kW 
products remains broad across conventional servers and lower power datacenter 
workloads. AES is working with several new CSPs and overseas SIs, with new 
customer contributions expected in 2027E. Rising GPU power, greater BBU 
penetration and higher content per MW should support volumes and mix-led ASP 
growth. Cell prices are rising, but AES indicates stable procurement through long-
standing relationships with suppliers and will seek to pass on cost increases. 
HVDC progress: commercialization likely in 2H27E — High voltage BBU 
commercialization has been delayed to 2H27E, reflecting product development and 
system integration challenges. Currently samples have been delivered and large-
scale testing is underway for 400V products, with mass production targeted for 
mid-2027E and 800V products are scheduled for end-2027E. Mgmt. sees high 
voltage BBU ASP to be 5-10x higher than low voltage products. We believe 
increasing system complexity and qualification requirements favor AES’s 
established engineering capabilities and CSP relationships, though shipment 
timing and margin contribution require monitoring.

Non-BBU development: new applications broaden the growth base — Mgmt
expects non-BBU growth to outpace BBU growth in 2027E as new projects scale.
LEV demand should gradually recover following the inventory correction. Telecom
application has entered mass production in 2026, with mgmt. expecting the
business to double in 2027E. AGV, AMR, forklift and robotics projects involve
leading international customers, with AGV/AMR products expected to enter mass

production in 2H27E. These projects should diversify revenue and extend AES’s
battery pack expertise into additional markets.
Earnings revisions and TP change —We lift our earnings forecasts by 6% for
2026E mainly to reflect stronger sales strength and GPM in 2026E while we keep
our 2027/2028E earnings estimates largely unchanged. We slightly lower our TP to
NT$1,550 based on 25x 2027E P/E vs. 27x prior reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty.

### Page 5

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
5
Advanced Energy Solution
Company description
Established in January 2020 and listed in March 2021, Advanced Energy
Solution Holdings (AES) is a holding company registered in Cayman Island,
supplying battery packs for LEV (light electrical vehicle such as e-Bikes) and
BBU (battery back-up unit for datacenters) applications. Prior to the
establishment and IPO, AES was a BU focusing on non-IT applications within

its parent company, Simplo Technology (6121.TW).
Investment strategy
We rate AES shares as Buy. Being an industry leader in battery pack design
and manufacturing with a proven track record, we expect AES to benefit from
higher BBU content per rack and deep CSP engagement as the industry
transitions from conventional 48V systems to HVDC architecture. BBU now
contributes >70% of sales and should remain the key earnings driver as AI
rack power continues to rise. We forecast earnings CAGR of 27% in 2025-

2028E, supported by strong BBU demand, mix upgrade toward high-power
BBU products and op leverage.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$1,550 is based on 25x 2027E EPS on par with its
historical average since listing and supported by a 2025-28E earnings CAGR
of 27%. We use 2027E as the year of valuation base as we think 2027E can
better reflect its earnings power after the commercializarion of HVDC
products.
Risks
Key downside risks to our earnings forecasts that could prevent the shares

from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected end demand
from e-Bikes post the pandemic and volaitlity in cloud capex deployment that
could impact datacenter BBU sales; 2) component supply
constraints/logistics challenges, which could lead to slower-than-expected
shipment and top-line momentum; and 3) peer competition, which could
impact sales growth and margins negatively, in our view.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
October  Sales +14% MoM/
+30% YoY
In this report, we focus on Delta's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• October sales came in at NT$74,666mn (+14% MoM/+30% YoY), driven by
the increasing shipment of power supply units, HVDC power racks, and liquid
cooling products.
• QTD revenue reached 35% of MSe at NT$215,881mn (+10% QoQ/+34% YoY),
and 34% of consensus at NT$219,806mn (+12% QoQ/+36% YoY).

Our view:
• We think 4Q26 revenue is tracking slightly ahead of market expectations.
• We think the slightly better Oct monthly sales should help on near-term
sentiment, though more meaningful upside to market expectations on
earnings growth might  depend on the penetration of HVDC power racks into
the coming years.
• We continue to see Delta as a key beneficiary of the increasing power
consumption of AI clusters through its comprehensive power/cooling

offerings. Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28e EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 59% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected global economic growth

n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 171: 261112_gs_inventec

### Page 2

Earnings revision 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 8% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 1%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 9%/8% to reflect product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 6/6%). We now expect the GM expansion 
to be slower due to the higher server business contribution that is margin dilutive, thus 
revise down GPM by 0.2/0.2/0.2ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Valuation:  
We reiterate our Neutral rating on Inventec with a new 12m TP of NT$56 (from NT$61), 
based on 14.2x 2027E P/E (unchanged), which is the company’s average P/E for the past 
10 years, and factoring in our earnings revision.

### Page 3

Investment thesis and Valuation & Risks

Inventec is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, servers, 
and consumer electronics. While we like Inventec’s solid position in the AI server ODM 
market, we are Neutral rated as we believe valuation already reflects near-term 
positives. We model Inventec earnings growth in 2026-28E to be driven by the 
commercial PC demand recovery and AI server ramp-up. We model AI servers to 
contribute more in 2026-28E. We see P/E valuation as fair at the current level compared 
to its peers and already reflects the solid growth in 2026-28E. Catalysts for further 
upside/downside would be its customers’ demand on AI servers and general server 
demand outlook.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$56 is based on 14.2x 2027E P/E, which is 
the company’s average P/E for the past 10 years. 
Key upside/downside risks: (1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
(2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and (3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영업이익은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아쉬웠습니다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추정치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 11 월 2 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 7

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

7 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계 
금융투자상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

## Document 253: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000 원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000 원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

## Document 302: COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses

Source PDF: all report pdf/COP31_ Why it still matters for businesses.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Is COP still worth following? We think so. What matters for
businesses is whether this year’s COP31 can improve the
business case for action. Here are four areas to watch

Coco Zhang and Gerben Hieminga
The global climate landscape has changed signiﬁcantly since world
leaders committed 11 years ago to limiting global warming to well
below 2°C and pursuing eﬀorts to limit warming to 1.5°C.
Geopolitical tensions, policy shifts, trade protectionism, rising
energy demand… they have all made it harder for governments to
turn climate commitments into action.
That raises an important question for businesses: Is the Conference
of the Parties (COP) still worth following?
Our answer is yes.
Businesses do not just invest in climate targets; they invest in viable
projects and operations. But many of them still face economic and
practical barriers. Grid constraints and high electricity costs can
slow electriﬁcation. Greener industrial products can struggle to
Your privacy is important

Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

attract buyers willing to pay a premium. And climate adaptation
plans do not always translate into investable projects.
Businesses cannot solve these challenges alone. In a 2025 UN
survey of more than 2,000 CEOs across 128 countries, 92% said
strong global governance and coordinated policy are “critical” or
“important” to implementing their sustainability agendas.
That is why COP is still relevant. It can help align policy priorities,
improving investment conditions, and connecting businesses with

the right resources. At COP31, four areas will be particularly
important for businesses to watch.
Electriﬁcation marries economic growth
with sustainability
Electriﬁcation is increasingly important for economic growth,
energy security, and sustainability. Demand from AI, EVs, heat
pumps and industrial decarbonisation is accelerating electricity
use, which is growing twice as fast as overall energy demand.
Electricity also punches above its weight economically, accounting

for 23% of global ﬁnal energy consumption but powering 46% of
global GDP. This implies that high value-added sectors rely more on
electricity than the overall economy.
Electricity’s economic contribution
outweighs its share of energy use

### Page 3

Source: ING Research, International Energy Agency
COP31’s push to accelerate electriﬁcation reﬂects this trend. The
COP31 presidency has set a goal of increasing electricity’s share of
ﬁnal energy consumption from 23% today to 35% by 2035.
Getting there will require addressing a major constraint: the grid. As
we have written, grid congestion could slow electriﬁcation, increase
costs, and delay electricity-dependent economic activity.
The key question at COP31 is how governments turn this ambition

into action. We think eﬀective action could include:
Establish a detailed electriﬁcation roadmap with interim
benchmarks to guide progress and sustain momentum across
future COPs
Enhance knowledge-sharing platforms. The International
Smart Grid Action Network has extensive experience sharing
best practices, but could go further by translating knowledge
into direct technical assistance and ready-to-deploy models
Set country- or sector-level targets and pathways to encourage

industry alignment and commitment
Improve access to ﬁnance by expanding dedicated ﬁnancing
for developing countries, while lowering upfront costs in Europe
Improve the economics. Coordinate energy pricing reforms to
make electriﬁcation more competitive with fossil fuel
combustion alternatives, including by reducing taxes and grid
fees that raise electricity prices
Strengthen infrastructure by encouraging governments to
reduce permitting barriers (e.g. Europe and the US), advance

### Page 4

regulatory support for power demand ﬂexibility, and expand
cross-border grids where needed (e.g. Southeast Asia)
If COP31 delivers meaningful action in these areas, the implications
for businesses could be signiﬁcant. Stronger policy could accelerate
near-term grid connections and enhance long-term investment
conﬁdence. Meanwhile, companies may need to work more closely
with utilities and regulators to secure the power they need.
Green industrialisation remains critical

But electriﬁcation is only part of the story. Industry is another
major source of process emissions, with sectors such as steel,
cement, and aviation accounting for roughly 20% of the global
total. Cutting these emissions is particularly diﬃcult because many
industrial processes still have few cost-eﬀective alternatives without
lengthy site overhauls.
COP31 has therefore put green industrialisation high on the
agenda, building on COP30’s progress. Following the Belém

Declaration on Global Green Industrialisation at COP30, a coalition
led by the Brazilian and UK governments mobilised hundreds of
millions of dollars in concessional ﬁnance for Brazilian industries
and awarded funding to 12 hydrogen projects. Over time, these
eﬀorts could reshape manufacturing processes and investment.
But many industrial decarbonisation technologies still face
signiﬁcant barriers to scaling. Their high cost premiums mean
economics depend heavily on government support. Demand for

cleaner industrial products remains limited. And the necessary
infrastructure for many is still underdeveloped.
Hard-to-abate sectors’ green premium is
way higher than what companies are willing
to pay
Diﬀerence in cost, low-carbon versus conventional products, in %

### Page 5

Source: World Economic Forum, ING Research
COP31 is not setting a new level of ambition for green
industrialisation. It’s focusing on implementing the existing
agenda. One priority is narrowing the demand gap. COP31 can
coordinate governments to create more demand for low-carbon
industrial products through long-term public procurement. If such
eﬀorts gain momentum at COP31, they could provide the long-run
revenue certainty investors need to back green industrial projects at

scale.
Climate ﬁnance faces multiple gaps
To realise the targets and priorities set forth at COP, investment is
crucial. And total ﬂows are growing. Global climate ﬁnance reached
around $2tn in 2024 and continued to grow in 2025, although at a
slower rate.
Global climate ﬁnance continues to grow

Source: Climate Policy Initiative, ING Research
But the headline number masks signiﬁcant gaps. First, even though
climate mitigation (i.e. emissions reduction) ﬁnance accounted for
$1.9tn of the $2tn total, the level falls well short of estimated
needs, which range from $7.6tn-$9.6tn through 2050. In areas such
as electriﬁcation and green industrialisation, funding is needed not
only for technologies, but also for the infrastructure and demand

needed to scale them.
Second, only $64bn of the total went exclusively to adaptation, an
area requiring surging investment because of intensifying climate
risks. Third, a substantial share of climate ﬁnance stays within
advanced economies or comes from domestic sources.
International ﬂows to help developing countries totalled only
$175bn, far below COP29’s target of $1.3tn per year by 2035.
Imbalances among growing global climate
ﬁnance

### Page 7

Source: ING Research, Climate Policy Initiative, UN, UNEP, Natural
Resources Defense Council
More climate products and solutions are emerging in the latter two
segments. But their higher risks or weaker business cases can make
it diﬃcult to attract capital without supportive policies. Investors are
actively turning to blended ﬁnance, which uses public capital to
help de-risk projects and mobilise private investment. Yet many still
lack the data, resources and connections needed to move projects

forward. This is where COP31 can help. As we discuss in the next
section, it can help governments create more favourable
investment conditions and connect suppliers, buyers and investors.
Adaptation is becoming a business
imperative and opportunity
Business awareness of climate adaptation has grown, as many
businesses are experiencing more disruptions ﬁrsthand. Our
previous analysis also shows how drought on Europe’s Rhine River,
hurricanes in the US, and other extreme weather events are already

aﬀecting supply chains across sectors. In aggregate, physical
climate risks could cost companies in the S&P Global 1200 index
$1.2tn annually by 2050.
One barrier to corporate investment in climate adaptation is that it
is still seen primarily as a way to avoid losses. But adaptation can
also create business value. Investments in resilient assets, cooling
systems, supplier diversiﬁcation and climate-resilient materials can
improve reliability, competitiveness and long-term growth.

Research from the World Resources Institute suggests that every $1
invested in adaptation can generate up to $10 in beneﬁts. That
return could be even higher for highly climate-exposed sectors. For
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies

### Page 8

example, the World Economic Forum estimates returns of $19–$43
per dollar invested in the food and beverage sector.
COP can help turn adaptation priorities into investable projects.
Since the Paris Agreement, countries have developed National
Adaptation Plans (NAPs), but many remain high-level and lack
speciﬁc, actionable projects. Recent COP eﬀorts have sought to
change this by encouraging governments to translate their NAPs
into portfolios of investable adaptation projects.

But identifying projects does not guarantee ﬁnancing. This is where
COP31 could make a diﬀerence. Its proposed Climate
Implementation Bridge (BRIDGE) initiative could make projects
easier for investors to identify and assess by centralising
information on technical requirements, risks, costs and beneﬁts.
Expanding the initiative to more countries could give more banks,
investors and companies access to potential adaptation
investments.
For businesses, a successful COP31 could therefore help unlock

capital for adaptation projects. The beneﬁts could extend
throughout supply chains, as companies increasingly depend on
the climate resilience of their suppliers to maintain reliable
operations.
Conclusion
The most eﬀective corporate sustainability strategies support long-
term growth, but they are more eﬀective with international
coordination and strong policy support. COP can bring
governments and businesses together to provide such a
framework, even amid geopolitical tensions and concerns over

slowing progress. COP31 may not deliver a landmark global
agreement, but practical advances in these four priorities could still
have immediate relevance for businesses.

## Document 358: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-14).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
New Zealand 2026 General Election:
Preliminary results and next steps.
9 Nov 2026 |  Westpac Economics Team | westpac.co.nz/economics | economics@westpac.co.nz
Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
The result.
The preliminary results of the 2026 General Election,
based on the election night count, are set out in the table
above. In summary:
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First and ACT
won 67 seats.
• The centre-left bloc of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti
Māori won 57 seats.
• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling and will
enter parliament with 7 seats.
These results indicate a swing away from National to its

coalition partners while the centre-left grouping picked
up a few seats. The big gainer was the Opportunity Party
who perhaps took a big share of what otherwise could
have been a larger swing to the centre-left grouping.
The size of parliament will expand by around nine seats,
as National held onto a large share of its electorate
votes and hence has more seats than it would have been
entitled to based on its party vote share. As a result, a
governing bloc would require 66 seats to command a

majority in parliament.
Several of the electorate seats are very tight (see Table
2), so election night majorities could easily be overturned
once special votes are counted. Of particular relevance
is the seat of Wairarapa, where the National candidate is
leading the Labour candidate by just 139 votes. If Labour
were to win that seat (specials favoured Labour in 2020
but National in 2023), all else equal the centre-right seat
count would decline to 66 seats in what would become

a 130-seat Parliament – just enough to command a
majority. But if either NZ First or ACT were to lose a single
party vote seat to the left bloc once specials are counted
then that majority would disappear.
On that score, historically the election night results have
tended to overstate the support of the centre-right bloc
and understate the support for parties on the left of
the political spectrum. For example, over the previous
three elections (2017, 2020, 2023), the current centre-

right bloc’s vote share has declined 1.9%, 1.7% and 1.6%
respectively following the counting of special votes, with
the National Party losing 2 seats on each occasion once
the official results were declared (Labour, the Greens and
Te Pāti Māori have benefited). On this occasion, given its
overhang, a lower party vote share would not lower the
National Party’s final vote count, although it could alter
the size of its overhang and thus the number of seats

required to command a majority. In recent elections both
NZ First and ACT have seen their vote shares decline
following the counting of special votes.
The process from here.
Official results.
The official results will be declared on 27 November,
adding the results of an estimated 400,000 special votes
(with around a quarter of these cast overseas) to the
2.41m votes counted on election night.
We expect discussions between potential coalition
partners will begin immediately to start sketching out the

parameters of a coalition agreement. But given the tight
margins of the current vote count, no firm positions will
be agreed until the results are declared.
The writ and recounts.
After 27 November, the writ is scheduled to be returned
on 3 December – formally certifying who has been
elected. However, that date is subject to any recounts

Westpac New Zealand Economics
that might be requested. Parliament is required to meet
no later than 14 January (six weeks after the return of the
writ), providing an opportunity for a new government
to test that it has the confidence of the House. It is
worth noting that Parliament can be summoned and
begin functioning even if coalition negotiations are still
occurring. If no coalition or confidence-and-supply
arrangement has been reached by then, negotiations can

simply continue and the incumbent Government remains
in office under the caretaker convention (Ministers in
the outgoing government retain executive authority
temporarily, even if they are no longer MPs).
Given that the Pre-Election Economic and Fiscal
Update was released in late September there is no legal
requirement for the Treasury to publish a Half Year
Economic and Fiscal Update (HYEFU) this year. And given
that it will be some weeks before a government is formed,

it seems unlikely that there will be a government in place
that would request that a HYEFU be produced despite
there being no statutory requirement (certainly there
would be little time to incorporate policy changes that
fall out of new coalition agreements, thus diminishing
its value). Once a government is formed, it will have an
opportunity to lay out its immediate priorities in the
Budget Policy Statement (BPS), which by law needs to
be released before the end of March next year. The new

government could decide to release an interim fiscal
update with the BPS if that was thought necessary.
Potential coalition outcomes.
We see three main likely outcomes:
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First.
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First with the support of the Opportunity Party
on confidence and supply.
• Some coalition of Labour, the Greens, Opportunity and
Te Pāti Māori, potentially with Opportunity and Te Pāti

Māori sitting outside the cabinet.
Our preview of the election discussed the policies of
the main political parties, while the figure on page 5
highlights some points of commonality with the centre-
right and centre-left blocs.
National, ACT and NZ First share a broadly pro-
development and deregulatory agenda, including support
for greater resource development, fewer regulatory
barriers and compulsory or expanded KiwiSaver. Beyond
that common core, National and ACT align particularly

closely on lower taxes and deregulation, while National
and NZ First share an emphasis on infrastructure, regional
development and law and order. ACT and NZ First favour
tighter immigration settings and tighter welfare policies.
The differences are clearest at the edges: ACT advocates
deeper tax cuts, smaller government and more market-
led provision, while NZ First places greater emphasis
on economic sovereignty, regional development and
targeted support for working New Zealanders. National

occupies more of the middle ground, combining fiscal
discipline and a pro-business agenda with increased
infrastructure and housing supply.
The Opportunity Party presents itself as a middle
alternative that can work with the left or the right
groupings. They have five bottom line policies they say
they need to support any new government. These include
competition reform through enhanced Commerce
Commission powers, more restrictions on political
lobbyists and donors, a ten-year plan of investment in the

healthcare sector, an end to bottom trawling in inshore
areas and a phase out elsewhere and for the Government
to recommit to New Zealand’s Paris Agreement
obligations. Their success in meeting these bottom
lines with a new government and their flexibility around
these requirements will be key to their ability to form an
agreement with either side. There is better congruence
of the Opportunity Party’s policies with the left than the
right – so progress here will be critical to watch should

their support be required to form a new government. We
suspect they will be very motivated to avoid the need for
a new election via a hung parliament should push come
to shove.

overlap particularly on higher taxation, public investment
and stronger climate and environmental policy, while

investment and higher public-service wages. The Greens
and Opportunity both favour broader taxation and
stronger climate action, although their preferred tax
instruments differ. Their distinctive priorities are also
clear: Labour places more weight on worker protections
and restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate; the Greens on
environmental protection, progressive taxation and Te
Tiriti; and Opportunity on land-value taxation, housing
infrastructure, productivity, innovation and evidence-

## Document 378: Sovereign debt_ When Short-Term Shocks and Shifts in Debt Structure Leave a Lasting Fiscal Burden — The Case of Brazil (2026-11-12)

SOVEREIGN DEBT: WHEN SHORT-TERM SHOCKS AND SHIFTS IN DEBT STRUCTURE LEAVE A LASTING FISCAL
BURDEN — THE CASE OF BRAZIL
Salim Hammad
Brazil’s recent post-election yield shock risks leaving a lasting imprint on public finances — compounded by already high interest rates and a
changing debt structure. Large refinancing needs and a rising share of floating-rate debt will transmit rapidly and significantly higher interest

rates to the debt service through distinct but reinforcing channels.
SOURCES : MACROBOND, BCB
Brazil’s presidential elections triggered a sharp repricing of Brazil’s rate outlook

CHART 1
An abrupt post-election market awakening

In the wake of the 2 nd round of the general elections in late October, Brazilian markets — which had expected a Flavio Bolsonaro
victory — sold off sharply after Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won a fourth presidential term. Both the equity and FX markets
experienced large intraday drops (14% of the Ibovespa stock index and -11% BRL vs. USD). Meanwhile, the yield on Brazil’s 10-year
government bond rose 250 basis points to 15.5%, reaching its highest level in over a decade (Chart 1) as investors worried about

the fiscal impact of costly campaign promises amidst large and persistent fiscal imbalances.
Fiscal risks have reshaped Brazil’s public debt structure, magnifying the cost of persistently high interest rates
Absent a confidence-infusing fiscal adjustment, Brazil’s interest bill is unlikely to decline in the short term, maintaining upward
pressure on public debt (83% of GDP in October 2026) and limiting budgetary leeway.
Three transmission channels, amplified by changing features of the debt structure, explain this persistence.

The first channel: The rise in long-term yields will increase rollover costs.
The sovereign must refinance roughly 20% of its debt stock each year. An average maturity of 4.1 years spreads refinancing over
time but does not shield the interest bill from a sustained rise in borrowing costs. As maturing securities are replaced, higher
issuance rates progressively lift the average borrowing cost — even for fixed-rate debt, whose interest costs remain unchanged

until refinancing. A sustained yield shock therefore feeds into debt servicing as the stock rolls over.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 10 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The second channel: Demand for floating-rate instruments will strengthen until confidence in fiscal policy improves.
In turn, a larger share of SELIC-linked debt heightens the debt stock’s sensitivity to the prevailing high policy rate. Already SELIC-linked
securities reached a record 52.6% of the debt stock in August 2026, around 12 percentage points above their share at the start of President

Lula’s last term, versus a low of 18.4% in September 2014 (Chart 2). Weaker demand for fixed-rate securities with maturities exceeding five
years, amid increased fiscal uncertainty, has encouraged this shift 1.
The third channel: The BCB may delay rate cuts until fiscal policy over the next mandate is better defined.
A slower easing cycle would prolong high interest costs on SELIC-linked debt, whose servicing costs adjust with the policy rate without

waiting for refinancing. With these securities accounting for a growing share of the debt stock, delayed monetary easing would postpone
relief for the Treasury’s interest bill — even if long-term market yields subsequently ease.
These channels operate at different speeds: a rise in long-term yields increases rollover costs, whereas floating-rate debt reprices with
the SELIC rate, not automatically with 10-year yields. Meanwhile, a slower monetary easing cycle and stronger demand for floating-rate

securities compound pressure on the interest bill.
The fiscal–monetary policy nexus is therefore likely to remain a source of strain on Brazil’s public finances: fiscal uncertainty could delay
monetary easing, while greater floating-rate exposure would prolong the burden of high policy rates on the interest bill.
salim.hammad@bnpparibas.com
1 Fiscal slippage and uncertainty make investors less willing to lock in nominal returns over long horizons: if inflation or market yields rise, the purchasing power of fixed payments

declines or the bonds’ market value falls. Investors therefore demand higher yields to bear this risk, making long-term fixed-rate funding more expensive for the Treasury. By issuing
floating-rate bonds instead, the Treasury offers returns that adjust with the SELIC rate, sustaining demand, securing financing and reducing the risk of failed auctions. However
longer floating-rate maturities may reduce rollover risk, but do not remove the repricing exposure. Floating-rate issuance helps secure financing but transfers interest-rate risk to

the Treasury.
SOURCES : NATIONAL TREASURY, BNP PARIBAS

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank of Thailand (BOT) (October 28)

We expect the BOT to maintain its policy 
rate at 1.00% at its 28 October meeting, 
extending its pause for a fourth 
consecutive decision as it balances 
inflationary pressures against uneven 
and subdued economic growth. Our view 
is aligned with fixed market income 
pricing. Headline inflation rose anew to 
2.8% yoy in September 2026, driven by 
higher energy, food, and core price 
increases, but remained within the upper 
end of the central bank’s 1-3% target 
range. As the BOT is still likely to look 
through what it views as temporary, 
supply-driven inflationary pressures, it 
will likely refrain from tightening policy, 
and keep interest rates accommodative 
to support the soft and uneven growth 
cycle.

European Central Bank (October 29)

We expect the ECB to extend its pause, 
keeping the deposit facility rate at 2.5%. 
Market participants will instead focus on 
the central bank’s assessment of rising 
French and Italian bond yields and 
widening spreads versus Bunds. While 
we do not expect direct references to 
individual countries, the ECB is likely to 
signal that recent developments in 
France reflect the country’s weaker 
fiscal and debt profile rather than a 
broader systemic concern. Policymakers 
will seek to differentiate between a 
localised bond sell-off and a Eurozone-
wide market disruption, justifying their 
decision to monitor developments rather 
than implement specific measures at this 
juncture. Meanwhile, inflation continues 
to rise, with little relief expected in 
upcoming readings given elevated 
global fuel prices. 
Bank of Japan (October 30)

The Bank of Japan is expected to keep 
the overnight call rate unchanged at 1.25% 
at this meeting, pausing after the 25bp 
hike in September. The BOJ is likely to 
assess the impact of the previous rate 
hike before taking further action. The 
recent stabilisation of the yen also 
reduces the urgency of another hike at 
this meeting.

The tightening bias should remain intact. 
Growth and inflation remain broadly on 
track, leaving room for the BOJ to revise 
up its FY2026–FY2027 GDP and CPI 
forecasts. Wage growth has also 
remained strong, with Rengo reportedly 
targeting wage increases of 6% in 2027, 
reinforcing the BOJ’s confidence that 
underlying inflation can be sustained 
around its 2% target. We continue to 
expect the policy rate to rise to 1.50% in 
December 2026 or January 2027, 
followed by a further increase to 1.75% 
by 2Q 2027.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Export growth is expected to accelerate 
from 53.0% yoy in August to 55.0% in 
September, in line with the pickup in 
China’s export growth from 25.0% to 
36.3%. As a key re-export hub for China,

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 23, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Hong Kong should benefit from robust 
AI-related electronics trade, with 
electrical machinery, equipment and 
parts exports to Hong Kong accounting 
for 20.6% of China’s total exports. 
Import growth is also expected to remain 
firm, supported by resilient export 
orders and gradually improving 
investment sentiment, sustaining 
demand for intermediate and capital 
goods.

Singapore

Singapore’s industrial production (IP) 
likely remained in expansion for a 13th 
consecutive month in September 2026, 
although we expect growth to moderate 
to 7.6% yoy from August’s strong 15.4% 
yoy pace. IP growth continued to be 
underpinned by the electronics and 
precision engineering clusters, which 
has been key drivers of Singapore’s 
manufacturing strength, reflecting 
sustained demand for artificial 
intelligence-related hardware. However, 
performance across clusters remained 
uneven, with chemicals and biomedical 
manufacturing likely contracting, and 
tempering overall gains.

South Korea

The preliminary estimate for 3Q26 GDP 
is likely to show growth remaining solid 
at 3.2% yoy, slightly below 3.7% in 2Q 
and 3.8% in 1Q. Nominal GDP growth is 
likely to remain in double digits, 
significantly outpacing real GDP growth, 
thanks to improved terms of trade amid 
rising memory export prices. By 
expenditure, exports picked up in 3Q in 
both nominal and real terms, supported 
by stronger semiconductor shipments. 
Investment also appeared robust, as 
reflected in the equipment investment 
estimation index and machinery order 
index. Private consumption, however, 
appeared to weaken amid the sharp 
KOSPI correction since July following 
the introduction of leveraged ETF 
regulations. Our full-year GDP growth 
forecasts of 3.5% for 2026 and 2.4% for 
2027 remain on track.

Taiwan

The preliminary estimate for 3Q GDP is 
likely to show growth remaining strong 
at 12–13% yoy, close to 12.9% in 2Q, 
albeit below the peak of 15.4% in 1Q. 
Exports maintained strong double-digit 
growth in 3Q (44.6% yoy in nominal 
terms and 21.1% in real terms), supported 
by robust shipments of semiconductors, 
servers and other AI-related products. 
Machinery investment also sustained 
strong double-digit growth, underpinned 
by higher capacity utilisation in the ICT 
sector and a resulting increase in capital 
goods imports. Private consumption 
appeared to have strengthened further, 
supported by stock market gains, 
improving wage growth, and the 
associated wealth and income effects. 
Construction investment remained the 
weak spot, weighed down by elevated 
inventories in the new-home market and 
the ongoing need for inventory digestion. 
We maintain our 2026 and 2027 GDP 
growth forecasts at 11.6% and 5.6%, 
respectively, with risks tilted to the 
upside.

## Document 427: “AI央行”失去掌舵人，华尔街警告：黄仁勋离开将引爆“AI版量化紧缩”！

Source PDF: all report pdf/“AI央行”失去掌舵人，华尔街警告：黄仁勋离开将引爆“AI版量化紧缩”！.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-14
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⻩仁勋骤然离场，令英伟达这台以股权投资、担保和回购构建的逾 5000 亿美元 AI 循环融资
机器失去操盘⼿。⾼盛、瑞银警告，其每季度⾃由现⾦流中⼤量资⾦循环输送回客⼾与供
应商，即便暂时暂停，也可能抽⾛ 500 亿美元以上资⾦，相当于触发 “AI 量化紧缩 ” ，新兴云
服务商信⽤链条⾸当其冲。
摘要：
英伟达董事⻓⻩仁勋的骤然离场，不仅是⼀家芯⽚公司的⾼管更迭，更是整个 AI 融资⽣态系统⾯
临的系统性冲击。在过去三年⾥，英伟达实际上已演变为 AI ⾏业的 " 最后贷款⼈ " ，以股权投资、
担保承诺和回购协议构建起⼀台规模逾 5000 亿美元的循环融资机器。如今，这台机器的操盘⼿
已经离场。
⻩仁勋辞职消息宣布后，英伟达股价盘中⼀度暴跌逾 13% ，收盘跌约 4% 。与此同时，⾼盛、瑞
银、 William Blair 等 14 家卖⽅机构相继下调评级或⽬标价，直指同⼀核⼼担忧：没有⻩仁勋的英
伟达，还能继续为 AI ⽣态系统提供资⾦输⾎吗？依赖英伟达资⾦的新兴云服务商（ neocloud ）

反应更为剧烈 ——CoreWeave （ CRWV ）当⽇下跌 11% ，⽽就在同⼀天，该公司正在市场上推
介⼀笔逾 40 亿美元的投资级贷款，英伟达据悉是该贷款的担保⽅。
CFO Colette Kress 在出任临时 CEO 的同时兼任 CFO ⼀职。华尔街正⾯临的核⼼问题已不是英伟
达下⼀个财季的业绩，⽽是：当这家公司 " 换脑 " 之后，那台为整个 AI ⾏业提供信⽤背书的融资机
器，是否还会照常运转？
卖出芯⽚、资助买家、托底房东：英伟达 5300 亿美元的循环逻辑
英伟达的融资版图，远⽐多数投资者意识到的更为庞⼤。截⾄今年 7 ⽉ 26 ⽇，英伟达持有的股权
投资规模达 990 亿美元，约为⼀年前的 13 倍；此外还有 1085 亿美元的担保承诺，以及 560 亿美元
的 AI 云回购协议和第三⽅租约。
这份投资名单⼏乎就是英伟达⾃⾝客⼾和供应商的花名册： AI 实验室⽅⾯，英伟达 3 ⽉向 OpenAI
投⼊ 300 亿美元，向 Anthropic 投⼊最多 100 亿美元， 7 ⽉向 Safe Superintelligence 投⼊约 50 亿美

元；新兴云服务商⽅⾯，英伟达 1 ⽉向 CoreWeave 投⼊ 20 亿美元， 3 ⽉为 Nebius 预付 20 亿美元认
股 权 证 ； 供 应 链 ⽅ ⾯ ， 英 伟 达 向 英 特 尔 投 ⼊ 50 亿 美 元 （ ⽬ 前 市 值 约 300 亿 美 元 ）， 还 向
Coherent 、 Lumentum 、 Marvell 和 Synopsys 各投⼊ 20 亿美元。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

其运作结构⼏乎⼀成不变：英伟达买⼊股权，受资⽅承诺采购英伟达硬件，资⾦以 GPU 订单的
形式回流。以 OpenAI 俄亥俄州园区项⽬为例，英伟达提供的担保上限为 1050 亿美元，附带 38 亿
⽡额外算⼒选项。英伟达⾃⼰的 CFO 注解点明了回报逻辑：该站点每⼀代英伟达基础设施的部
署，⼤约对应 150 万块 GPU ，折合约 1500 亿⾄ 2000 亿美元的英伟达营收 —— 换⾔之，英伟达⽤
1050 亿美元的租⾦担保，换取每个升级周期⾼达 2000 亿美元的潜在芯⽚收⼊，周期⻓达 20 年。
将英伟达 10-Q 报告脚注中的全部承诺加总，可以得出以下数字：3660 亿美元的未来承诺（其中
供应与产能采购承诺为 2790 亿美元，仅三个⽉内即从 1190 亿美元激增）、560 亿美元的 " 额外 " 承
诺，以及 1085 亿美元的担保 —— 三项合计 5305 亿美元。这还不包括 990 亿美元的股权账簿，以
及⻩仁勋今年 8 ⽉联合阿波罗、⻉莱德、⿊⽯、布鲁克菲尔德、⾼盛和 KKR 推出的 5000 亿美元融

资平台中，英伟达承诺最⾼可提供 25% 兜底、即 1250 亿美元的部分。
对于这台融资机器的本质， Panmure Liberum 曾⼀针⻅⾎地指出：所谓 " 英伟达银⾏ " 既是这家芯
⽚公司的护城河，也是有限⾃由现⾦流被⼤量押注在⼀个仍在苦苦寻找变现路径的⾏业上所积
累的信⽤⻛险。
⾼盛、瑞银相继预警： "AI 版量化紧缩 " 的传导路径
⻩仁勋离场后，华尔街关注的焦点已从英伟达⾃⾝，快速转向其融资收缩对整个 AI ⽣态的连锁效
应。
⾼盛分析师 James Schneider 维持买⼊评级，但将⽬标价从 300 美元下调⾄ 220 美元，他在报告
中写道：英伟达已作出的承诺将会兑现，问题在于下⼀轮。在没有⻩仁勋的个⼈愿景、⼈脉和
召集⼒的情况下，新的多⽅⽣态系统投资将更难推进。⾼盛还指出，英伟达股票此前早已以显
著折价交易，部分原因正是市场已在为 " 供应商融资停⽌后 AI 基础设施增⻓骤然放缓 " 定价。
瑞银分析师 Tim Arcuri 在当晚发布的初步分析中，将融资问题列为三⼤核⼼影响的⾸位。他估

计，英伟达每季度约 500 ⾄ 600 亿美元的⾃由现⾦流中，有相当⼤⼀部分被循环输送回客⼾、供
应商和新兴云服务商，即便只是暂时性的暂停，也可能在关键时刻从 AI ⽣态系统中抽⾛ 500 亿美
元以上的资⾦ —— 这相当于由⼀份新闻稿触发的 "AI 量化紧缩 " 。
William Blair 分析师 Sebastien Naji 将英伟达降级⾄ " 市场表现 " ，其结论是：现有合同不受 CEO
更迭影响，但下⼀轮交易岌岌可危，⽽这直接关系到英伟达能否在 2027 年及以后守住市场份
额。与此同时，布罗德康（ Broadcom ）等竞争对⼿有望乘虚⽽⼊ ——Broadcom 已分别为
Anthropic 的两笔芯⽚融资交易提供了⾼达 72.5% 的兜底⽐例，⽽英伟达的融资平台最⾼只给
25% 。

### Page 3

500 亿美元融资平台颗粒⽆收，各⽅已要求重议条款
据彭博报道，⻩仁勋今年 8 ⽉⾼调发布的 5000 亿美元融资平台，在与六⼤资产管理机构联合亮相
三个⽉后，迄今尚未落地任何⼀笔交易。彭博报道显⽰，英伟达此前向市场提供的 " 残值⽀持 " 上
限为交易规模的 25% ，但在过去⼀个⽉内，部分客⼾和华尔街合作⽅已要求其提⾼兜底⽐例，
理由是 Broadcom 能给出更慷慨的条件。按最乐观的估计，该平台最早也要到明年 1 ⽉才有可能
宣布⾸笔交易，且这⼀时间表还取决于⻩仁勋的离职不会令相关谈判脱轨。
在这⼀背景下，市场参与者已开始就条款重新讨价还价。 Raymond James 全球私⼈资本顾问主
管 Sunaina Sinha Haldea 对 CNBC 表⽰，管理层更迭为所有相关机构提供了⼀个天然的暂停节
点，各⽅会重新审视交易条款；她预计资本不会就此撤⾛，但 " 他们可能会要求更⾼的不确定性
溢价 " ，这意味着整个英伟达资产负债表下游的信⽤利差将全⾯⾛阔。
Saxo ⾸席投资策略师 Charu Chanana 则警告称，在债务规模不断膨胀、循环融资结构和不确定

回报本已受到市场质疑的背景下，更⼤的⻛险在于 AI 融资领域的涟漪效应 ——
" ⿅死于猎灯之下 " ： Kress 接⼿⼀台正在减速的机器
据《华尔街⽇报》报道，今年 8 ⽉，⻩仁勋将核⼼财务⾼管召⾄办公室，直⾯⼀个⽇益失控的问
题：英伟达正在就向根本负担不起其芯⽚的企业提供数⼗亿美元担保进⾏谈判。部分交易由⻩
仁勋的⼥婿、今年 1 ⽉晋升为副总裁的 Nico Caprez 主导，他曾向新兴云服务商和数据中⼼开发
商承诺，英伟达可以在数周内安排信⽤⽀持，换取对⽅的收⼊分成，数⼗家公司随即蜂拥⽽
⾄。
就在这次会议上，⻩仁勋当着众⼈的⾯对 Kress 的汇报⼤加批评，打断她的陈述，称其为 " ⿅死于
猎灯之下 " （ a deer in the headlights ），评价她只是⼀位汇报型 CFO ，⽽⾮能够参与战略定义的
⾼管 —— ⽽公司董事会如今决定让她扮演的，恰恰是后⼀⻆⾊。
然⽽，从历史记录来看，Kress 恰恰是整个扩张狂潮中少数持续追问 " 这些担保究竟值多少钱 " 的
⾼管。据《华尔街⽇报》、路透和 The Information 报道：是她将 OpenAI 的初始承诺⾦额腰斩；

是她在幕后试图约束 Caprez 的交易冲动；也是她提出了那些在狂热⽓氛中⽆⼈愿意回答的问题
—— 如何筛选交易、如何为担保定价、如何计算回报。
今年 10 ⽉，英伟达已开始逐步收紧融资⽔⻰头，每笔新交易均须经⾼管团队和董事会批准。但
外部压⼒正从多个⽅向同时袭来 —— 美国司法部正就客⼾芯⽚出货情况展开调查，众议院委员
会主席要求进⾏闭⻔汇报，⽽公司的永久董事⻓⼀职尚悬空待填。
新兴云服务商的脆弱链条：担保重定价的第⼀波冲击
英伟达的担保之所以价值连城，根本原因在于它能将信⽤质量低下的借款⼈包装为投资级债务
发⾏⼈。⾼盛的分析框架显⽰，在 CoreWeave 的延迟提款定期贷款结构中，客⼾的信⽤评级直
接决定了 SPV 能够承载多少债务、以及以何种利率融资。当英伟达的信⽤背书填补了中间的评
级空缺，原本是垃圾级的新兴云服务商才得以按投资级利率融资。
据 The Information 报道， Nscale 为其德克萨斯州站点获得了 8.6 亿美元的租⾦担保，并正在与英
伟达谈判超过 50 亿美元的信⽤⽀持，⽤于其为 Anthropic 建设的西弗吉尼亚州园区 —— ⽽英伟达

的担保正是 Nscale 能够完成融资的前提。
"这还不是融资危机，但它可能成为 AI ⽣态系统⻛险重定价的催化剂。 "

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

⼀位知情⼈⼠向《财富》表⽰，" 如果资本提供⽅现在认为英伟达的担保已不再值⼀周前的价
格，那些新兴云服务商的业务就会陷⼊危机。 "⾼盛 "Top of Mind" 报告亦刊载了 Chamath
Palihapitiya 对新兴云服务商的悲观判断：这类公司的盈亏平衡利⽤率约在 70% ⾄ 75% ，⽽当前
普遍处于 75% ⾄ 85% 的灰⾊地带 —— 在这⼀区间，它们虽然盈利，但所得不⾜以在硬件过时之
前偿清债务。什么都不需要变得更糟，这⼀结局就会⾃然到来。
现实已初现端倪。澳⼤利亚的 Firmus 在今年 10 ⽉先削减后撤回了其 IPO 计划， CoreWeave 的欧
元债券仍以约 80 美分的价格交易。⼀旦英伟达真正收紧担保，最先受到冲击的将是债务负担最

重的那些借款⼈。
11 ⽉ 17 ⽇（周⼆）， Kress 将以临时 CEO 兼 CFO 的⾝份主持英伟达第三季度财报电话会议。届时
必然有分析师追问那 5000 亿美元的问题。她的答复，将告诉市场 " 英伟达银⾏ " 究竟是继续开⻔营
业，还是已悄然转⼊存量贷款的维护模式。

分析師／謝智偉

市場投資展望
《消費量能未見失速訊號》
• 9月卡片消費YoY回升至+6.2，
顯示量能尚未失速
1
資產 4Q26 看法 投資展望
核心消費 中立 本行維持產業上下半年營收成長48：52的溫和成長預期，消費產業在旺
季前呈現「量能可望持平、獲利品質分化」，同時零售商備貨行為與終
端消費需求仍支持到貨量的基本盤，但消費者實際支出意願可能受到油
價／利率上升抑制，戰術性減碼受油價與信心承壓較重的旅遊休閒類股
4Q26預計呈「量能可望維持、獲利偏弱」格局
資料來源：美國銀行2026/10/15，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。

• 消費量能未見失速訊號 P.1
• 「K型」分化持續收斂 P.2
• 4Q26傳統銷售旺季Bottom-Up P.3
• 核心消費三個次產業需求驅動各異 P.4
• 成本與定價動能 P.5
消費產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/29
BofA信用卡數據9月卡片消費YoY回升、零售銷售續增(惟
剔除汽油站僅+0.2%),延續8月自7月疲弱中的反彈,為本行
對量販/民生通路保持加碼提供量能基準。
總卡片消費YoY成長率於6-8月連續降溫(6月6.3%→7月
5.0%→8月4.5%),剔除油品後同步走緩
(5.6%→4.3%→3.7%)，主因6月世界盃與線上促銷時序錯
位帶來的高基期效應消退。9月YoY回升至6.2%、剔除油品
為5.1%(BofA指出皆為近四年第三強),惟季調後MoM僅
+0.2%(7月-0.2%、8月+0.9%),剔除油品後幾乎持平,顯示
YoY反彈部分來自油價推升,月度動能趨於溫和。投資意涵：
量能未見明顯失速，不支持對消費產業全面削減曝險,宜改
採結構性次產業輪動

重點摘要
零售客流量表
• K型分化大致收斂。低所得家戶
的消費與稅後薪資成長自7月起
皆居三分位（Tercile）組之首，
高所得（不含前5%）已被中、
低所得超越。
• 驅動力分歧，脆弱點也不同。
中低所得消費有收入基礎，但

於薪資成長，依賴財富效果。

《「K型」分化持續收斂，中低所得消費有收入基礎》
低所得家戶的消費與稅後薪資成長自7月起皆居三分位
（Tercile）組之首，高所得（不含前5%）已被中、低所
得超越。三分位組中，低所得家戶的消費與薪資成長皆最
強；前5%仍是消費成長最快的族群，但已較高點放緩。
各組消費成長均高於稅後薪資成長約1個百分點（pp）。

重點摘要
零售客流量表
5
民生必需品：P&G有機成長 +4.0%中，銷量貢獻 +2.4%，
價格貢獻有限；引用的NielsenIQ資料也顯示家庭與個人
護理類 +4.3%、包裝食品 −0.9%。成長主要來自銷量與
組合而非漲價，且品類間分化明顯，這是本行僅給予中性
評等的主因。
餐飲：2Q26同店銷售呈現明顯分化，本行預估客流領先
的連鎖與咖啡系統維持正成長，倚賴折價促銷的速食與披
薩鏈許多為負成長。在外食價格比居家食品高出約1.7個
百分點的環境下，客流與價格的取捨將是4Q26餐飲評等
的主要變數。
《成本與定價動能》
9月CPI顯示，整體CPI月增 +0.6%、年增 +3.7%，核心
CPI年增 +2.6%；能源年增 +20.4%、汽油年增 +34.2%，

是整體通膨上行的主因。對核心消費而言，關鍵在於食品
與能源的分離：居家食品月增 −0.1%、年增 +1.8%，外
食年增 +3.5%。
對毛利的含意：能源成本上升而零售端食品價格溫和，代
表核心消費業者較難以轉嫁成本，壓力更可能先反映在毛
利率而非銷售額。P&G在指引中揭露本財年約14億美元
稅後成本逆風（含其他逆風合計約每股0.79美元，約拖累
核心EPS成長11%），為公司自行揭露數字，本行未重新
計算。Kimberly-Clark則預期2026年有機銷售成長約落
後 所 屬 品 類 加 權 成 長 100bp ， 顯示在品類成長僅約
+1.7% 的環境下價格與組合的操作空間有限。成長動能
偏向「量」而非「價」；此結構有利於營收穩定，但對毛
利率的支撐較弱。

◼ 金融市場：半導體和AI相關類股受挫，日台韓

熱門議題/資產影響推估 市場焦點
➢ 美國國會變天：民主黨不僅重新奪回眾議院，也
以遠超預期的幅度拿下參議院多數席次。整體政
策方向其實不會發生重大改變，但民主黨未來可
能利用財政法案與債務上限談判，推動限制對伊
朗戰爭、部分撤銷關稅以及影響財政支出。
➢ 市場焦點放在資料中心建置進程：AI資料中心的
建設是否受到影響，是目前市場最關注的焦點。
本行認為短期逆風確實存在，但不影響長期建置
趨勢。

美國國會變天，AI概念受挫然未見風險情緒，殖利率回落
AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊
盤勢回顧
美10年期利率回到5%以下，信用利差自高點收斂
3
• AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊：民主黨全面掌控國會後，市場擔憂AI產業、大型雲端服務與資料中心的監
管與審查將顯著收緊，使相關類股、日台韓股市均下挫，不過資金輪動到其他產業，支撐美股指數維持穩定。
• 財政紛擾仍存但支出展望中性，美10年債利率來到5%以下：市場開始反應財政政策可望趨於中性，再加上地
緣政治有望緩和，布蘭特原油跌破100美元/桶，支持美國公債利率持續走低至5%以下，自10月高點以來下滑
近50個基點。殖利率下滑輔以金融市場不確定因素降低支撐股市反彈，同步帶動美國信用債利差收斂，11月以
來美國信用債總報酬緩步走揚。

兩黨對立將放大財政期限影響與紛擾，整體財政支出趨於中性
兩黨對立，財政期限的重要性上升
政策影響
2027年的財政動能預計將大致維持中性
4
• 財政期限的重要性上升，帶來政治紛擾：民主黨可利用具強制性的時間節點，作為政策讓步的槓桿。第一個要
注意的時間點是12/11政府支出授權到期，不過將期限延到2027年2至3月的機率較高(否則政府可能關門)。另
外推論1Q27將臨近債務上限，以目前的財政部存款和非常規措施的使用，最多可支應至2027年8月至10月。
• 財政政策整體趨於中性：分裂政府通常意味著較不具擴張性的財政政策，而眾議院掌握聯邦預算的撥款權，將
可能透過拒絕追加或直接限制戰爭預算的方式來迫使戰事降溫。然民主黨同時也可能與共和黨協議，以撤銷去
年大而美法案的支出削減措施、恢復健康保險補貼等作為交換條件，讓白宮得以增加國防預算。綜合來看，短
線面臨預算協調的紛擾，美債殖利率波動仍可能偏高，但趨勢上政策制衡效果增強，有利減輕殖利率的壓力。

5
• 民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化可能有限：民主黨領袖已表示將嘗試撤銷已實施的部分關稅，但
川普政府仍可利用301條款推動新的關稅，整體有效關稅稅率變化可能有限，但其中可能的重點是降低對加拿大
的關稅。
• 對資料中心監管程度將提高，不影響建置趨勢：傳統共和黨票倉的德州變天，而德州是最大的資料中心建設地
區，新任的民主黨州長可能要求企業自行承擔電力及電網擴建成本、保障居民用水優先權，並在保護規範明確
之前影響資料中心的建設進度。不過本行認為，除了要求企業揭露資料來源與訓練資訊、建立安全治理架構，
以及落實其他風險管理措施等規定之外，尚無太多州級監管制度被認為具有高度限制性。而在聯邦層級，大規
模的新監管措施即使被國會提出，也很可能被川普否決，且AI競爭力仍是跨黨派國策，建設趨勢將持續推進。
資料來源：左， 高盛 ，2026/10，右，高盛，2026/10。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

### Page 6

©  CTBC
AI 資料中心已從「產業政策」轉變為「民生電價議題」
超大型業者算力供給轉吃緊
產業影響
2030年電力缺口收斂
6
• 德州資料中心暫停令，AI算力布局的轉變與供需影響：德州8月初頒布的暫停令，同步凍結了新資料中心的興建
（僅限未動工新案）與電力輸送。在扣除被淘汰的虛報專案需求後，預估部分專案將外溢至其他電力更吃緊的州，
導致全美電力缺口淨擴大約 3–6 GW。若Hinojosa 就任後延續暫停令執行超過一年，其影響2028 年全美電力缺
口將進一步擴大，2030年供需缺口預估將逐步收斂。另一方面，暫停令讓2028年本就吃緊的算力市場更緊，維
持算力租金的高檔區間，對超大型業者而言建置成本增加，與手上既有的算力可以提高售價，兩者相抵，2028
年企業獲利的預估影響僅在1-3%水準。
• 提高監管是正在發生的實質逆風，喊停是低機率的極端政治口號：AI 技術攸關美國的核心基本國力。民主黨的
政策向來主張「調整與監管」，而非全面「廢止」。因此，未來更嚴格的許可審查與補助限制，勢必會提高雲

端大廠在德州擴張的時間成本與資本風險。然而，也可能推動針對資料中心的特定稅種，例如聯邦總收入稅，
或針對資料中心電力消耗的消費稅。民主黨也有可能在新會期提出比共和黨更強制性更嚴格監管的版本。民主
黨掌控國會後，最可能成法的是「電費成本分攤」立法，因為川普已將此議題列為自己的政績。

健護最具兩黨協商的價值，醫療產業相對受惠
12/11為醫療補貼可以放進談判的時間點
產業影響
健護未來一年的本益比仍合理
7
• ACA補貼及醫療補助是最可能與川普達成交易的領域：民主黨贏得參眾兩院，不等於民主黨執政，兩黨仍須協
商。就健護產業而言，相對有利影響的領域在2025年底已到期的<平價醫療法案(ACA)>增強型保費補貼，受淨保
費上升，已有超過200萬人放棄投保，及受<大而美法案>被削減的醫療補助(Medicaid)在2025~2034年超過
9,000億美元。現ACA進行增強型補貼的恢復提案還在進行中，需總統簽署才能立法恢復，可以是談判交換條件
的一部分；Medicaid刪減是已成法、分階段生效，無法被推翻，只能延緩Medicaid的刪減，其中對工作的要求
限制於今年底生效，早於新國會就任，12/11臨時撥款法案談判是可以納入談判作為局部修正空間的時間點。
• 健護是民主黨國會最直接的受惠產業，健護未來一年本益比仍相對合理：預期若醫療補助刪減延後，醫院將受
惠，ACA補貼若部分恢復，將有利ACA型管理式醫療業者的投保人數止跌回升。以健護未來一年本益比來看，

目前股價仍相對合理。

資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢

市場觀點
資料來源：(左) 高盛，2026/11；(右) 摩根大通，2026/11 。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11
分裂國會在兩年任期內的表現優於單一政黨執政
8
1974年以來標普500指數期中選舉前後的走勢
• 期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢：根據歷史經驗，期中選舉年通常是總統任期中回撤較深的一年，且選
前的股市波動會明顯放大，然在選後不確定性消退，市場表現往往有所改善，標普500指數在選後的3個月，歷
史中位數報酬率為+6%，而分裂政府(總統和國會分屬不同黨派)的歷史報酬率通常較佳。
• AI相關類股波動高，資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢：真正驅動市場的因素仍是利率、地緣、
AI，而不是單純從歷史規律推演，近期市場擔憂雲端業者的現金流和債務問題，因此若資料中心的建置時程受
到影響，將進一步影響相關業者的營收和償債時程，提高AI相關類股的波動；另外眾議院是否對川普提出彈劾，

也將造成政治上的波動。但就整體趨勢來說，既有政策不會因此出現重大轉折，穩健成長的企業獲利提供重要
支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢。

©  CTBC
短線待消化北亞出口疑慮，長線行情回歸基本面
情緒回穩後聚焦基本面與地緣動盪 短線消化資料中心建設監管趨嚴對北亞出口影響，
然長線美核心政策延續性依舊，估計影響有限
9
• 選後反彈高度取決景氣與政策推演：回顧過去美股期中選舉期間，新興市場選前壓抑，選後大多繳出正報酬佳
績，上漲比率超越五成。以參眾兩院控制權皆翻轉的1994年、2006年來看，1944年因美聯準會啟動快速升息，
加上墨西哥金融危機爆發，選後新興市場進入近一年空頭走勢。2006年即便也是分裂政府，全球宏觀環境受惠
貿易繁榮、商品超級周期推動經濟成長，新興市場選後12個月累積報酬亮眼。總體來說，選後不確定性下降，
新興市場可望迎反彈走勢，而即便美國政治版圖翻轉，選後新興市場走勢取決於景氣、政策推演與地緣干擾。
• 市場短線待消化資料中心建設進程對北亞出口影響：美期中選舉對新興市場影響較大面向，主要是關稅與貿易
政策、債務上限，以及對資料中心建設的態度。關稅主導權在白宮推估影響有限 ; 預算與債限談判或對利率造
成波動 ; 短線需消化選後對資料中心監管趨嚴影響北亞出口的失望情緒，然預計長線AI建置仍屬國家重心政策。

• 期中選舉共和黨大敗，美元走弱：週三市場確認共和黨於期中選舉失利後，美元兌主要貨幣全面回檔。本次期
中選舉由民主黨取得參、眾兩院多數。民主黨掌控參議院後，可阻止或延後川普的人事任命；贏得眾議院後，
則可對政府展開調查，甚至進一步推動彈劾程序。歷史經驗顯示，當民主黨於期中選舉後同時掌控參、眾兩院
時，美元指數平均下跌4.6%，6次中有4次下跌。整體而言，選舉結果短期內可能因預算協商及債務上限僵局

升高政治風險。2027年美國將再度面臨債務上限問題，美伊地緣政治及貿易戰等變數亦將牽動實質利率走勢。
• 2027年債務上限恐成兩黨鬥爭焦點，不利美元：12月11日前，現任國會通過持續撥款決議的可能性偏高，但
美國聯邦政府收支預計將於2027年觸及債務上限。受債務上限約束的債務規模預估於2027年3月接近法定上限
500億美元以內，之後隨4月稅收入帳而暫時下降；但若財政赤字增幅高於預期，X-Day恐提前到來。展望
2027年，美國財政議題仍將面臨逆風。共和黨失去完全執政雖有助抑制財政擴張，但政治僵局恐加劇債務上限、
違約及信評遭調降風險，使美元波動加大。

[ニュージーランド]～雇用環境が厳しさを増すなか、７-９月期の失業率は 11 年ぶりの高水準に～ 
４日に発表された７-９月の失業率（季調済）は 5.7％となり、前期（5.6％）から 0.1pt 悪化し
て 2015 年７-９月以来、11 年ぶりの高水準となった。失業者数は前期比＋0.3 万人と前期（同＋0.8
万人）に続いて２四半期連続で増加しており、中期的にも増加基調が続く動きが確認されている。
一方、就業者数は前期比▲0.5 万人と前期（同＋1.0 万人）から５四半期ぶりの減少に転じており、
持ち直しの動きに一服感が出ている。業種別では、世界的なＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体関連需要の旺
盛さを追い風とするデータセンター関連投資の堅調さが建設業の雇用を下支えする一方、製造業で
は雇用拡大の動きに一服感がみられるとともに、サービス業でも幅広い業種で頭打ちの動きが確認

### Page 2

2/5 
されている。雇用環境の悪化を受けて労働力人口も前期比▲0.2 万人と前期（同＋1.8 万人）から
５四半期ぶりの減少に転じており、労働参加率も低下するなど、労働市場への参加の動きが弱まっ
ている。さらに、賃金の伸びも鈍化しており、イラン情勢の悪化に伴うエネルギー価格の上昇がイ
ンフレ圧力を強めるなか、家計の実質購買力が下押しされるなど、個人消費を取り巻く環境は厳し
さを増している。

[韓国]～AI･半導体需要を追い風に輸出の拡大が続く一方、サービス物価を中心にインフレ圧力くすぶる～ 
１日に発表された 10 月の輸出額は前年同月比＋93.7％となり、前月（同＋83.7％）から伸びが
加速した。当研究所が試算した季節調整値に基づく前月比もプラスとなっており、旺盛な動きが続
いている。財別では、世界的なＡＩ（人工知能）・半導体関連需要の旺盛さを反映して、主力輸出財
である半導体関連の輸出が引き続き堅調に推移するとともに、コンピュータ関連の輸出も大幅に拡
大している。国・地域別でも、最大の輸出先である中国向けのほか、米国向けやＥＵ（欧州連合）
向け、ASEAN（東南アジア諸国連合）をはじめとするアジア新興国向けも軒並み大幅に拡大してい
る。一方、輸入額は前年同月比＋36.0％となり、前月（同＋26.0％）から伸びが加速した。前月比
も小幅ながら２ヵ月連続で増加したと試算されるなど、持ち直しの動きが続いている。輸出の堅調
さを反映して半導体製造装置の輸入が押し上げられたほか、素材・部材関連の輸入も堅調に推移す
るとともに、原油などの商品市況の上昇も輸入額を押し上げている。その結果、貿易収支は＋425.01
億ドルと前月（＋499.72 億ドル）から黒字幅が縮小した。 
３日に発表された 10 月の消費者物価は前年同月比＋2.8％となり、前月（同＋2.9％）からわず
かに伸びが鈍化した。前月比も＋0.25％と前月（同＋0.32％）から上昇ペースが鈍化しており、原
油価格の高止まりを受けてエネルギー価格は上昇基調が続く一方、生鮮食品をはじめとする食料品
価格の上昇に一服感がみられるなど、生活必需品の物価動向にばらつきが生じている。なお、食料
品とエネルギーを除いたコアインフレ率は前年同月比＋2.8％となり、前月（同＋2.8％）から横ば
いで推移しており、総合インフレ率とともに中銀目標（２％）を上回る水準にある。前月比は＋
0.39％と前月（同▲0.09％）から２ヵ月ぶりの上昇に転じており、エネルギー価格の上昇に伴い輸
送コストに押し上げ圧力がかかる一方、金融市場におけるウォン相場の強含みを受けて輸入物価の
上昇が抑えられるなど、財価格は比較的落ち着いた動きをみせている。他方、コスト上昇分の価格
転嫁を反映してサービス価格を中心に上昇する動きが確認されており、基調的なインフレ圧力は依

[インドネシア]～インフレ率は鈍化するも物価上昇圧力は根強く、輸出は過去最高を更新するなど堅調～ 
１日に発表された 10 月の消費者物価は前年同月比＋3.23％となり、前月（同＋3.28％）からわ
ずかに伸びが鈍化した。前月比は＋0.22％と前月（同＋0.30％）から上昇ペースこそ鈍化したもの
の、３ヵ月連続で上昇している。エネルギー価格が２ヵ月ぶりの下落に転じる一方、生鮮食品をは
じめとする食料品価格は上昇が続いており、生活必需品の物価動向にはばらつきがみられる。なお、
コアインフレ率も前年同月比＋2.56％となり、前月（同＋2.84％）から伸びが鈍化しており、総合
インフレ率とともに中銀の目標レンジ（2.5±１％）内に収まっている。一方、前月比は＋0.12％と
前月（同＋0.10％）からわずかに上昇ペースが加速しており、輸送コストへの上昇圧力に加え、金
融市場におけるルピア安を受けた輸入物価の押し上げ圧力も重なり、幅広い財の価格が上昇する動
きがみられる。さらに、コスト上昇分の価格転嫁を反映してサービス価格も上昇基調で推移するな
ど、基調的なインフレ圧力は依然として根強い様子がうかがえる。 
また、同日に発表された９月の輸出額は前年同月比＋11.87％となり、前月（同＋6.72％）から伸
びが加速した。当研究所が試算した季節調整値に基づく前月比も３ヵ月ぶりのプラスに転じており、
単月ベースの輸出額は過去最高を更新している。財別では、同国政府による事実上の輸出規制策を
受けて天然ガスの輸出額が下振れするとともに、原油の輸出額も頭打ちしている。一方、鉱物資源
関連や農産品の輸出額が底堅い動きをみせているほか、製造業関連の輸出も堅調に推移しており、
輸出全体を押し上げている。他方、輸入額は前年同月比＋18.41％となり、前月（同＋19.09％）か
ら伸びが鈍化した。ただし、当研究所が試算した季節調整値に基づく前月比は３ヵ月ぶりのプラス
に転じており、持ち直しの動きがみられる。原油や石油製品関連の輸入額が堅調に推移するととも
に、機械製品関連の輸入も底堅い動きをみせている。その結果、貿易収支は＋33.57 億ドルと前月
（＋35.52 億ドル）から黒字幅が縮小した。

[台湾]～インフレ率、コアインフレ率ともに伸びは鈍化も、幅広い品目でインフレ圧力が強まる動き～ 
５日に発表された 10 月の消費者物価は前年同月比＋2.33％となり、前月（同＋2.74％）から伸
びが鈍化した。前月比も＋0.06％と前月（同＋0.45％）から上昇ペースが鈍化しており、電力料金

料品価格が下落に転じるなど、生活必需品を中心に物価上昇圧力が和らいだことが影響している。
生鮮食料品とエネルギーを除いたコアインフレ率も前年同月比＋2.23％となり、前月（同＋2.53％）
から伸びが鈍化した。一方、前月比は＋0.54％と前月（同＋0.07％）から上昇ペースが加速してい
る。ガソリン価格の上昇などに伴う輸送コストの増加に加え、金融市場における台湾ドル安を受け
た輸入物価の押し上げ圧力も重なり、幅広い財の価格が上昇しているほか、コスト上昇分の価格転
嫁を反映してサービス価格も上昇するなど、インフレ圧力が広がる動きがみられる。

## Document 497: 不用Flash命名，谷歌发布Gemini 4 Hydrogen：幻觉减少5倍，速度翻倍

Source PDF: all report pdf/不用Flash命名，谷歌发布Gemini 4 Hydrogen：幻觉减少5倍，速度翻倍.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⾕歌发布 Gemini 4 Hydrogen ，定位⾼效执⾏引擎，主打速度与准确性。相较上代 Flash ，
任务速度提升 2.5 倍，幻觉率降低逾 5 倍。该模型已全球上线，覆盖搜索、 Gemini 应⽤及
API 等多平台。⾕歌同步推出 Orchestrator 功能，动态调度 Carbon 与 Hydrogen 协作，降低
24% 配额消耗。
摘要：
⾕歌以⼀款⾯向普通⽤⼾和开发者的⾼效能模型，全⾯扩⼤ Gemini 4 系列的覆盖半径。
继本周推出旗舰模型 Carbon 之后，⾕歌周四发布 Gemini 4 Hydrogen ，以 " 为所有⼈带来前沿智
能 " 为核⼼定位。
该模型主打速度与执⾏效率，放弃沿⽤的 "Flash" 品牌命名，以 Hydrogen 取⽽代之。
⾕歌表⽰， Hydrogen 已⾯向开发者、企业和消费者全球同步上线，覆盖 Gemini API 、 AI
Studio 、 Gemini 应⽤、⾕歌搜索及 Google Antigravity 等多个平台。
在基准测试⽅⾯， Hydrogen 在 Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index 上以默认⾼思考模式超越同

系列的 Argon 模型（ 51 分对 50 分），开启最⾼ xhigh 思考模式后可达 54 分。
与上⼀代 Gemini 3.8 Flash 相⽐， Hydrogen 在同等设置下完成任务速度提升 2.5 倍， Terminal-
Bench 4.1 得分从 18.2% 跃升⾄ 57% ，幻觉⽣成率（ fabricated claims ）降低逾 5 倍。
对开发者⽽⾔，该模型定价为每百万输⼊ token 1.5 美元，每百万输出 token 7.5 美元，价格在全
部 100 万 token 上下⽂窗⼝内统⼀计算。
定位：效率执⾏层，补⻬多模型协作拼图
⾕歌对 Gemini 4 系列的分⼯定位已趋于清晰：Carbon和Argon承担深度推理与复杂任务，
Hydrogen则扮演快速执⾏引擎的⻆⾊，适⽤于⽇常编码、智能体⼯作流及多步骤数据处理。
为将这⼀多模型协作模式系统化，⾕歌同步在 Google Antigravity 中推出 "Orchestrator" 功能。该
功能在同⼀会话中动态调度 Carbon 与 Hydrogen ，根据任务复杂度⾃动切换，⽆需⽤⼾⼿动⼲

预。
⾕歌披露的内部测试数据显⽰， Orchestrator 在 Terminal-Bench 4.0 上接近 Carbon 的任务成功
率，同时平均减少 24% 的配额消耗，速度提升 19% 。
在并⾏智能体⼯作流中， Carbon 可担任协调者， Hydrogen 则驱动⼯作与验证智能体，在相互隔
离的⼯作空间中同步构建与测试代码变更 —— 旨在帮助团队在控制 token 成本的同时加速复杂项
⽬交付。
消费端：搜索与 Gemini 应⽤获全⾯升级
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

在⾯向普通⽤⼾的产品层⾯， Hydrogen 已成为⾕歌搜索 AI Mode 的默认模型，同时⽤于 AI
Overviews 中 " 最难问题 " 的处理。
⾕歌表⽰，Hydrogen 能够更好地理解⽤⼾的真实意图，即便查询语句存在多个⼦问题、难以辨
认的拼写错误或⾼度具体的需求，搜索系统也能智能解析。此外，搜索结果将更具视觉化呈
现，并涵盖更⼴泛的相关链接。
在 Gemini 应⽤中， Hydrogen 在模型选择器中被定位为 " ⽇常助⼿ " ，处于 " 快速回答 " （ 3.5 Flash-
Lite ）与 " 深度研究 " （ 3.1 Pro ）之间。免费⽤⼾可在提⽰适配时，通过 "[Auto] ⾃动模型选择 " 机
制使⽤ Hydrogen 。
⾕歌表⽰，该模型能更严格地遵循⽤⼾设定的语⽓、格式与规则，使邮件草稿、学习指南及⽂
档在⾸次⽣成时即可直接使⽤。
在 Google Antigravity 中， Hydrogen 新增 "Extra" 思考层级，付费的 AI Pro 和 Ultra 订阅⽤⼾可使⽤

该功能，并可接⼊上述 Orchestrator 多模型协作能⼒。

### Page 3

性能：速度与准确性双重跃升
⾕歌将 Hydrogen 定位为对 3.8 Flash 的 " 重⼤独⽴升级 " ，核⼼改进集中于速度、准确性和上下⽂
处理能⼒三个维度。
在速度⽅⾯，合作伙伴早期测试数据显⽰， Hydrogen 在复杂图计算、 SQL 及数据密集型企业⼯
作流中运⾏速度提升 2 倍⾄ 2.8 倍，同时 token 使⽤量减少 12% ⾄ 23% 。
企业软件公司 Box 的 VP Yashodha Bhavnani 表⽰， Hydrogen 在准确性显著提升的同时，运⾏速
度约为 3.8 Flash 的两倍， token 使⽤量减少 12% 。 Yashodha Bhavnani 称：
在准确性⽅⾯， Hydrogen ⽀持原⽣ 100 万 token 输⼊与最⾼ 100 万 token 输出，能够处理密集的
PDF ⽂件、电⼦表格、图表、⾳频和视频内容。
在多模态知识智能体（ MKA ）基准测试中， Hydrogen 以 53.4% 的得分超越其他模型；在⾯向端
到端专业⼯作流的 Agents' Last Exam v1.1 测试中，得分达 71.6% 。

在垂直领域， Hydrogen 在 HLE-Diamond 深度领域推理测试中以 58.8% 领先同级模型，在 Vals
Tax Agent Bench 中以 52.3% 排名第⼀，在 Vals Legal Research Bench 中以 55.8% 并列第⼀。
⽣物技术公司 LatchBio 的团队表⽰， Hydrogen 在其抗体开发基准测试的毒理学与安全部分通过
率达 67% ，⽽⾮ Gemini 模型仅为 37% 。
安全性：智能体对⻬能⼒⼤幅强化
在需要准确读取表格和执⾏复杂计算的数据密集型⼯作流中，改善最为明显。

务不⾜ " （ under-service ）降低最⾼ 79% ，在⼯具调⽤和代码评估中的错位⾏为与任务⾛捷径

⾯对⽆法完成或被阻断的任务时， Hydrogen 绕过权限边界的概率降低最⾼ 2 倍，在⾕歌内部智能
体诚实性评估中达到 96.7% 的诚实率。
在推理透明度⽅⾯， Hydrogen 在逾 20 万次审计对话中保持 99.9% 的思维链可读性，且⽆任何欺
骗性推理编辑记录。

在针对间接提⽰注⼊攻击的 Gray Swan IPI 基准测试中， Hydrogen 的抗攻击能⼒提升逾 2.3 倍。
此外，⾕歌表⽰，更新后的安全护栏在识别真实⽣物安全⻛险与合法医疗或科学问题⽅⾯精度
显著提升，在 HealthBench Pro 上的误拒率低于 0.4% 。

## Document 512: 中期経済見通し（2026～2036年度）

Source PDF: all report pdf/中期経済見通し（2026～2036年度）.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 27
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

（備考）2025年度までの実績は内閣府「国民経済計算」により作成。

１. 世界の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は、2022 年以降３％台半ばで推移しているが、少子高齢化を背
景とした新興国の成長鈍化により、予測期間の終期の 2036 年には２％台後半まで低下す
ると予想する。 
２. 日本の実質ＧＤＰ成長率は、2036 年度までの先行き 10 年間の平均で 0.9％と予想する。
潜在成長率は足下のゼロ％台後半から 2020 年代後半に１％程度まで高まった後、少子高
齢化の更なる進展に伴い 2030年代前半にゼロ％台後半まで鈍化すると見込む。 
３. 消費者物価上昇率（生鮮食品を除く総合）は、先行き 10 年間の平均で 2.1％と予想する。
企業等の各経済主体の期待物価上昇率は２％前後となってきている。中東情勢の悪化に
よる物価上昇が落ち着くにつれ、2028 年度以降は、飲食料品の消費税率の引下げ・引上
げの影響を除けば２％程度の安定的な物価上昇が実現すると見込む。 
４. 日本銀行は、当面は物価上昇が再燃する中、消費者物価上昇率を２％程度に安定させてい
くため、段階的に利上げを実施し、政策金利は 2027 年度に1.75％まで引き上げられると
見込む。長期金利は、長期国債の買い入れ減額の継続に加え、国内の貯蓄超過の縮小が上
昇圧力になることから、2036 年度には３％台後半まで上昇すると予想する。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-10-13

中期経済見通し（2026～2036 年度）

経済研究部 
(03)3512-1884

### Page 2

主要先進国の成長率はコロナ禍で大きく落ち込んだものの、その後は相次ぐショックにもかかわ
らず底堅く推移している。ホルムズ海峡の輸送が制約される中では、世界的な原油在庫の取り崩し、
エネルギー需要抑制や政府主導での節約措置、供給経路・供給源の多様化（中東産原油の代替ルー
トでの輸送や中東以外の原油増産）などが供給制約ショックを吸収している。 
インフレ率は 2022 年のエネルギー価格高騰などを背景に急上昇したのち、2025 年にかけて緩や
かに低下してきた。しかし、コロナ禍前の水準まで低下しないまま、中東情勢の悪化で再び上昇圧
力が強まっている。2022年のインフレ加速期に、主要中央銀行による金融引き締めが遅れた経験も
あり、中央銀行はインフレ警戒感を強めており、利上げに転じる国も増えつつある。

（ＡＩ関連需要が成長率を押し上げ） 
成長率の底堅さの要因として、足もとで顕著となっているのが世界的なＡＩ関連需要の高まりで
ある。半導体など電子部品需要、データセンターを中心とした建設需要、電力などのインフラ投資
需要が増加している。世界半導体売上高（3か月移動平均）は急増しており、2026 年5 月以降、前
年比で 2倍を超えている。米中を中心にＡＩ開発が急速に進んでいるほか、ＡＩ関連需要は貿易を
通じて特にアジアの生産・輸出を押し上げるなど、世界に波及している。

### Page 3

こうした状況下、主要先進国では長期金利の上昇圧力が強まっている。インフレ圧力の強まりと
中央銀行の利上げ観測のほか、政府の財政支出拡大懸念や民間部門での資金調達需要が金利の押し
上げ圧力となっている。 
財政支出に関して、米国では主要財源と見られた関税引き上げ政策のうち、最高裁判所により国
際緊急経済権限法（ＩＥＥＰＡ）に基づく関税が無効と判断され、歳入の不透明感が増した。欧州
ではトランプ政権による安全保障政策の方針転換を背景に防衛費を拡大する動きが進む。日本では、
ガソリン価格抑制策など物価高対策が長期化するなか、「責任ある積極財政」を掲げる高市政権の
財政支出に注目が集まっている。民間部門の資金調達需要の高まりは、巨大ＩＴ企業によるＡＩ関
連投資の拡大などが背景にある。

（経済を取り巻く不確実性は引き続き高い） 
世界経済を取り巻く不確実性は高い状態が続いている。トランプ大統領の講じる政策は予見可能
性が低い。今後も通商領域やその他の領域で国家間の摩擦が強まる可能性がある。短期的な経済シ
ョックが生じうるだけでなく、中長期的にはトランプ大統領の米国第一主義や既存の国際秩序や多
国間枠組みを見直す政策姿勢がドル資産への需要やドルの基軸通貨としての地位に影響を及ぼす
可能性もあるだろう。地政学的リスクも大きく、すでにウクライナや中東地域では地政学的要因が
経済に悪影響を及ぼしている。原油在庫の取り崩しや供給源の多様化といったバッファーには量的

### Page 4

（世界の実質経済成長率は緩やかに低下） 
世界経済は、新型コロナウイルス感染症の影響で 2020 年に▲2.7％の大幅なマイナス成長となっ
た後、2021年はその反動で 6.7％の高成長となった。その後は、ロシアによるウクライナ侵攻、米
国による関税率の大幅な引き上げ、中東情勢の悪化など、相次ぐショックに見舞われてきた。しか
し、世界経済はこうした逆風のなかでも底堅さを保ち、実質経済成長率は 2022年以降、3％台半ば
で推移している。中期的には予測期間にわたって緩やかな鈍化傾向をたどり、予測期間末の2036 年
には 2％台後半まで低下することが見込まれる。 
先行きの成長率を先進国と新興国に分けてみると、新興国の成長率は先進国を一貫して上回ると
みられる。しかし、少子高齢化に伴い潜在成長率の低下が進むことなどを背景に、新興国の成長率
寄与度は予測期間後半に 2％台前半まで低下すると予想する。

世界経済に占める新興国の割合（ドルベース）は、2000 年の 20％程度から足もとでは 40％程度
まで上昇している。新興国の成長率は今後緩やかに低下するものの、先進国を上回る成長が続くこ
とから、その割合は予測期間末には 40％台半ばまで高まるだろう。

### Page 5

国別にみると、世界第 2 位の経済規模を持つ中国は、2018 年に経済規模でユーロ圏を上回るな
ど、コロナ禍前まで世界経済に占める割合を拡大させてきた。しかし、先行きについては、中国の
名目成長率が予測期間後半にかけて緩やかに鈍化し、人民元の対ドル相場が横ばい圏にとどまるこ
とから、予測期間中は米国の経済規模が中国を一貫して上回ると見込まれる。 
中国に匹敵する人口を抱えるインドは、予測期間中は人口増加が続くことから高い潜在成長率を
期待でき、世界経済に占める割合を高めていくとみられる。2020 年代後半にはインドの経済規模が
日本を上回ることが予想される。 
一人当たりＧＤＰ（ドルベース）をみると、日本は 1980年代後半から 1990 年代にかけて米国を
上回っていたが、2000 年頃にその関係が逆転した後は一貫して米国を下回っている。日本の一人当
たりＧＤＰは円安の影響もあり、2025 年には米国の 4 割程度の水準まで低下した。今後 10 年間の
日本の平均成長率は米国を下回ることが予想されるため、予測期間終盤も日本は米国の 4割弱の水
準で推移することとなるだろう。また、ユーロ圏と比較すると、2025年時点で日本の一人当たりＧ
ＤＰはおよそ 7 割であるが、予測期間の平均成長率がユーロ圏を下回るため、予測期間後半には 6
割程度まで低下するだろう。 
一方、日本のＧＤＰの水準は 2010年に中国に抜かれたが、一人当たりＧＤＰでみれば 2025 年時
点でも中国の2.5 倍程度の水準にある。今後は中国の成長率が日本を大きく上回るが、予測期間末
でも日本の一人当たりＧＤＰは中国の 2倍程度の水準を維持するだろう。また、予測期間中盤に日
本のＧＤＰを抜くインドは、一人当たりＧＤＰでみれば 2025 年時点では日本の約 7％となってい
るが、10年後には 11％程度の水準まで緩やかに上昇するだろう。
